Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on acoustics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 757. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 508 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to acoustics structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 757/508 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the acoustics structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 676. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 35 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to acoustics theory, we decompose the problem into 676 independent subproblems. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
